    <script>
        // Admin Module - SECURE & ENHANCED
        const Admin = {
            isLoggedIn: false,
            currentUser: null,
            // Pre-hashed passwords (SHA-256)
            // admin1: 'pass1', admin2: 'pass2'
            users: {
                'admin1': 'e6c3da5b206634d7f3f3586d747ffdb36b5c675757b380c6a5fe5c570c714349',
                'admin2': '1955798993c9d7224269866299f061405e60e87dcb4275034d97645391e604f5'
            },
            newsData: [
                {
                    title: "Penerimaan Peserta Didik Baru (PPDB) Gelombang 1 Dibuka!",
                    img: "uploads/JoinUs.mp4",
                    date: "5 Feb 2026",
                    author: "admin99",
                    excerpt: "BP3NU Raden Rahmat mengumumkan pembukaan pendaftaran Gelombang 1 untuk unit MI, SMP, dan SMA... Segera daftarkan putra-putri Anda sebelum kuota terpenuhi.",
                    link: "https://www.instagram.com/osis_smprrahmat/reel/DD7OaGwPOvv/"
                },
                {
                    title: "Pelantikan OSIS SMP R Rahmat",
                    img: "pelantikan osis.webp",
                    date: "15 Jan 2026",
                    author: "Humas",
                    excerpt: "Prestasi membanggakan kembali diraih oleh ananda Ahmad dari kelas IX yang berhasil menyisihkan puluhan peserta...",
                    link: "https://www.instagram.com/osis_smprrahmat/p/DR1k0yYkSdM/"
                }
            ],

            async init() {
                // Determine API Path
                this.apiPath = 'api/news.php';
                this.miApiPath = 'api/news_mi.php'; // MI news feed
                this.uploadPath = 'api/upload.php';
                this.authPath = 'api/auth.php';

                // AI article cache initialization
                this._articleCache = this._articleCache || new Map();

                // For normal visitors, DO NOT fetch or render news dynamically.
                // The PHP cron job (`inject_berita.php`) has already written the 
                // fully-expanded AI articles directly into the DOM as static HTML!

                // Only load data into state silently so the Admin Editor has it ready 
                // just in case they log in. 
                try {
                    const cb = `?cb=${Date.now()}`;
                    const response = await fetch(this.apiPath + cb);
                    if (response.ok) {
                        const data = await response.json();
                        if (Array.isArray(data) && data.length > 0) {
                            this.newsData = data;
                            // Note: We deliberately DO NOT call this.renderNews() here!
                        }
                    }
                } catch (e) {
                    console.log("Silent fetch failed:", e);
                }

                // ── STEP 4: Merge MI feed in background ────────────────────────────
                try {
                    const cb = `?cb=${Date.now()}`;
                    const miResponse = await fetch(this.miApiPath + cb);
                    if (miResponse.ok) {
                        const miData = await miResponse.json();
                        if (Array.isArray(miData) && miData.length > 0) {
                            const currentNews = this.newsData || [];
                            const shortcodeSet = new Set();
                            currentNews.forEach(item => {
                                const sc = this.extractInstagramShortcode(item.link || '');
                                if (sc) shortcodeSet.add(sc);
                            });
                            let added = 0;
                            miData.forEach(item => {
                                const sc = this.extractInstagramShortcode(item.link || '');
                                if (!sc || !shortcodeSet.has(sc)) {
                                    currentNews.push(item);
                                    if (sc) shortcodeSet.add(sc);
                                    added++;
                                }
                            });
                            if (added > 0) {
                                this.newsData = currentNews;
                                // We strictly DO NOT call this.renderNews() to prevent overwriting AI HTML!
                            }
                        }
                    }
                } catch (e) { /* MI fetch failed silently */ }


                // ── STEP 5: Check Session Status ───────────────────────────────────
                fetch(`${this.authPath}?action=check`)
                    .then(res => res.json())
                    .then(authData => {
                        if (authData.logged_in) {
                            this.isLoggedIn = true;
                            this.currentUser = authData.user;
                            this.userRole = authData.role || 'admin';
                            const infoEl = document.getElementById('admin-info');
                            const bannerEl = document.getElementById('admin-banner');
                            if (infoEl) infoEl.innerText = `MODE ${this.userRole.toUpperCase()} AKTIF (${this.currentUser})`;
                            if (bannerEl) bannerEl.style.display = 'block';
                            this.renderNews(); // Re-render to show edit buttons
                        }
                    }).catch(e => { });

                // Add preview listeners
                const fileEl = document.getElementById('file-upload');
                const imgEl = document.getElementById('edit-img');
                if (fileEl) fileEl.addEventListener('change', (e) => this.handleFilePreview(e.target.files[0]));
                if (imgEl) imgEl.addEventListener('input', (e) => this.updatePreview(e.target.value));
            },

            normalizeNewsItem(item) {
                const normalized = { ...item };
                const mediaType = String(normalized.media_type || '').toLowerCase();
                const isVideo = normalized.is_video === true || mediaType === 'video' || this.isVideoMedia(normalized.video || normalized.img || normalized.link);
                normalized.is_video = isVideo;
                return normalized;
            },

            enforceAutoplay() {
                // UX IMPROVEMENT: No more multiple autoplay videos!
                // Only show play overlay, user must click to play

                const videoContainers = document.querySelectorAll('.news-img-box');

                videoContainers.forEach(container => {
                    const video = container.querySelector('video');
                    if (!video) return;

                    // Remove autoplay attributes
                    video.removeAttribute('autoplay');
                    video.removeAttribute('loop');
                    video.removeAttribute('playsinline');
                    video.removeAttribute('preload');
                    video.setAttribute('preload', 'metadata');

                    // Add play overlay if not exists
                    if (!container.querySelector('.play-overlay')) {
                        const overlay = document.createElement('div');
                        overlay.className = 'play-overlay';
                        overlay.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-play"></i>';
                        container.style.position = 'relative';
                        container.appendChild(overlay);
                    }

                    // Click to play
                    container.addEventListener('click', () => {
                        // Pause all other videos first
                        document.querySelectorAll('video').forEach(v => {
                            if (v !== video) {
                                v.pause();
                                v.controls = false;
                            }
                        });

                        // Toggle play/pause
                        if (video.paused) {
                            video.play().catch(e => console.log('Play blocked:', e));
                            video.controls = true;
                            container.querySelector('.play-overlay')?.style.display = 'none';
                        } else {
                            video.pause();
                            video.controls = false;
                            container.querySelector('.play-overlay')?.style.display = 'flex';
                        }
                    }, { once: false });
                });
            },

            // === NEW UX FUNCTIONS ===

            truncateText(text, maxLength) {
                if (!text || text.length <= maxLength) return text;
                return text.substring(0, maxLength) + '...';
            },

            toggleArticle(articleId, index) {
                const fullArticle = document.getElementById(`full-article_${articleId}`);
                const btn = document.getElementById(`readmore_${articleId}`);

                if (!fullArticle || !btn) return;

                const isExpanded = fullArticle.style.display !== 'none';

                if (isExpanded) {
                    // Collapse
                    fullArticle.style.display = 'none';
                    btn.innerHTML = 'Baca Selengkapnya <i class="fas fa-chevron-down"></i>';
                    btn.classList.remove('expanded');
                } else {
                    // Expand
                    fullArticle.style.display = 'block';
                    btn.innerHTML = 'Tutup <i class="fas fa-chevron-up"></i>';
                    btn.classList.add('expanded');

                    // Trigger AI generation when expanding
                    const newsItem = this.newsData[index];
                    if (newsItem) {
                        this.generateAIArticle(articleId, newsItem);
                    }
                }

                // Smooth scroll to content
                fullArticle.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
            },

            shareToWA(title, link) {
                const text = encodeURIComponent(`*${title}*\n\nBaca selengkapnya: ${link}\n\nDari BP3NU Raden Rahmat Balongbendo`);
                window.open(`https://wa.me/?text=${text}`, '_blank');
            },

            shareToFB(link) {
                window.open(`https://www.facebook.com/sharer/sharer.php?u=${encodeURIComponent(link)}`, '_blank', 'width=600,height=400');
            },

            copyLink(link) {
                navigator.clipboard.writeText(link).then(() => {
                    alert('Link berhasil disalin ke clipboard!');
                }).catch(err => {
                    console.error('Gagal menyalin link:', err);
                    // Fallback
                    const textarea = document.createElement('textarea');
                    textarea.value = link;
                    document.body.appendChild(textarea);
                    textarea.select();
                    document.execCommand('copy');
                    document.body.removeChild(textarea);
                    alert('Link berhasil disalin!');
                });
            },

            loadMoreNews() {
                this.displayedCount = Math.min(this.displayedCount + 5, this.totalCount);
                this.applyLoadMore();
                this.updateLoadMoreButton();
            },

            applyLoadMore() {
                const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.news-card');
                cards.forEach((card, index) => {
                    if (index >= this.displayedCount) {
                        card.style.display = 'none';
                    } else {
                        card.style.display = 'flex';
                    }
                });
            },

            updateLoadMoreButton() {
                const btn = document.getElementById('load-more-btn');
                const container = document.getElementById('load-more-container');

                if (!btn || !container) return;

                const remaining = this.totalCount - this.displayedCount;

                if (remaining <= 0) {
                    container.style.display = 'none';
                } else {
                    container.style.display = 'block';
                    btn.innerHTML = `<i class="fas fa-plus"></i> Muat Lebih Banyak (${remaining} artikel lagi)`;
                    btn.disabled = false;
                }
            },

            // Switch Upload Tabs
            switchUpload(mode) {
                const btnLocal = document.getElementById('btn-local');
                const btnUrl = document.getElementById('btn-url');
                const divLocal = document.getElementById('upload-local');
                const divUrl = document.getElementById('upload-url');

                if (mode === 'local') {
                    btnLocal.style.background = '#e6f4ea'; btnLocal.style.color = '#006837';
                    btnUrl.style.background = '#fff'; btnUrl.style.color = '#666';
                    divLocal.style.display = 'block';
                    divUrl.style.display = 'none';
                    this.uploadMode = 'local';
                } else {
                    btnLocal.style.background = '#fff'; btnLocal.style.color = '#666';
                    btnUrl.style.background = '#e6f4ea'; btnUrl.style.color = '#006837';
                    divLocal.style.display = 'none';
                    divUrl.style.display = 'block';
                    this.uploadMode = 'url';
                }
            },

            uploadMode: 'local', // default

            handleFilePreview(file) {
                if (!file) return;
                const reader = new FileReader();
                reader.onload = (e) => {
                    this.updatePreview(e.target.result);
                };
                reader.readAsDataURL(file);
            },

            updatePreview(src) {
                const container = document.getElementById('img-preview');
                if (!src) {
                    container.innerHTML = '<span style="color: #ccc; font-size: 0.8rem;">Preview Media</span>';
                    return;
                }

                // Basic check if video or image
                // Note: 'src' from FileReader includes MIME type. URL doesn't necessarily.
                // We'll assume image for URL unless it ends in .mp4

                const isVideo = src.match(/data:video/) || src.endsWith('.mp4') || src.endsWith('.webm');

                if (isVideo) {
                    container.innerHTML = `<video src="${src}" style="width:100%; height:100%; object-fit:contain;" controls muted></video>`;
                } else {
                    container.innerHTML = `<img src="${src}" style="width:100%; height:100%; object-fit:contain;">`;
                }
            },

            // Utility: SHA-256 Hashing
            async hash(string) {
                const utf8 = new TextEncoder().encode(string);
                const hashBuffer = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', utf8);
                const hashArray = Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer));
                const hashHex = hashArray.map((bytes) => bytes.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
                return hashHex;
            },

            // Helper to check media type for rendering
            renderMedia(item) {
                const rawVideo = item?.video || '';
                const rawImage = item?.img || this.buildInstagramMediaFallback(item?.link || '');
                const cleanVideo = this.normalizeUrl(rawVideo);
                const cleanImage = this.normalizeUrl(rawImage);
                const isVideo = item?.is_video === true || this.isVideoMedia(cleanVideo || cleanImage || item?.link || '');
                const isExternalInsta = (url) => !!url && (
                    url.includes('scontent') ||
                    url.includes('cdninstagram') ||
                    url.includes('fbcdn') ||
                    url.includes('instagram')
                );

                const shortcode = this.extractInstagramShortcode(item?.link || '');
                const proxify = (url) => isExternalInsta(url) ? `api/image_proxy.php?url=${encodeURIComponent(url)}${shortcode ? `&shortcode=${shortcode}` : ''}` : url;

                let proxiedImage = proxify(cleanImage);
                let proxiedVideo = proxify(cleanVideo);

                // If is_video but we have no actual video URL cached (only a thumbnail),
                // show the cached thumbnail image with a play-icon overlay instead of
                // a broken proxy-video request that resolves to a 1×1 GIF.
                if (isVideo && !cleanVideo) {
                    const imgSrc = proxiedImage ||
                        (shortcode ? `api/image_proxy.php?shortcode=${shortcode}&kind=image` : '');
                    const instaLink = item?.link || '#';
                    if (!imgSrc) {
                        return '<div style="width:100%;height:100%;background:#eee;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;color:#ccc;"><i class="fas fa-play-circle fa-2x"></i></div>';
                    }
                    return `<a href="${instaLink}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="display:block;width:100%;height:100%;position:relative;">
                        <img src="${imgSrc}" alt="News Media" width="220" height="140" style="width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;" onerror="this.src='https://via.placeholder.com/400x250/006837/ffffff?text=Video+Thumbnail'; this.onerror=null;">
                        <span style="position:absolute;top:50%;left:50%;transform:translate(-50%,-50%);background:rgba(0,0,0,0.55);border-radius:50%;width:46px;height:46px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;pointer-events:none;">
                            <i class="fas fa-play" style="color:#fff;font-size:1.2rem;margin-left:3px;"></i>
                        </span>
                    </a>`;
                }

                // We have an actual video URL — render a proper <video> element
                if (isVideo && (proxiedVideo || cleanVideo)) {
                    const src = proxiedVideo || cleanVideo;
                    const posterSrc = proxiedImage || '';
                    return `<video src="${src}" poster="${posterSrc}" width="220" height="140" autoplay loop muted playsinline preload="metadata" class="autoplay-video" onloadedmetadata="this.play()" onerror="this.classList.add('hide'); let nx=this.nextElementSibling; if(nx){nx.classList.remove('hide'); nx.src='${posterSrc || 'https://via.placeholder.com/400x250/006837/ffffff?text=Video+Not+Available'}';} "></video><img src="${posterSrc || 'https://via.placeholder.com/400x250/006837/ffffff?text=Video+Not+Available'}" alt="News Media" width="220" height="140" class="hide" onerror="this.src='https://via.placeholder.com/400x250/006837/ffffff?text=Image+Not+Available'; this.onerror=null;">`;
                }

                if (!cleanImage && !shortcode) {
                    return '<div style="width:100%; height:100%; background:#eee; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; color:#ccc;"><i class="fas fa-image fa-2x"></i></div>';
                }

                // Use shortcode resolver as ultimate fallback for images
                const imgSrc = proxiedImage || (shortcode ? `api/image_proxy.php?shortcode=${shortcode}&kind=image` : '');
                return `<img src="${imgSrc}" alt="News Media" width="220" height="140" loading="lazy" onerror="this.src='https://via.placeholder.com/400x250/006837/ffffff?text=Image+Not+Available'; this.onerror=null;">`;
            },

            buildInstagramMediaFallback(link) {
                if (!link || typeof link !== 'string') return '';
                const shortcode = this.extractInstagramShortcode(link);
                if (!shortcode) return '';
                return `https://www.instagram.com/p/${shortcode}/media/?size=l`;
            },

            extractInstagramShortcode(link) {
                if (!link || typeof link !== 'string') return '';
                const match = link.match(/instagram\.com\/(?:p|reel|tv)\/([^\/\?\#]+)/i);
                return (match && match[1]) ? match[1] : '';
            },

            isVideoMedia(url) {
                if (!url) return false;
                // Common extensions
                if (url.match(/\.(mp4|webm|mov|ogg|m4v)/i)) return true;
                // Instagram reels are videos
                if (url.includes('instagram.com/reel/') || url.includes('/reel/')) return true;
                // CDN / Social media markers - check for actual video files
                if ((url.includes('instagram') || url.includes('fbcdn')) && url.match(/\.(mp4|webm|mov)/i)) return true;
                // Data URIs
                if (url.startsWith('data:video/')) return true;
                // Check for media_xxx.mp4 style from local uploads
                if (url.includes('uploads/') && url.endsWith('.mp4')) return true;
                return false;
            },

            normalizeUrl(url) {
                if (!url) return '';

                // If it's a full localhost URL, extract the relative path
                if (url.includes('localhost') || url.includes('127.0.0.1')) {
                    // Extract everything after /radenrahmat/
                    const match = url.match(/\/radenrahmat\/(.+)/);
                    if (match && match[1]) {
                        return match[1];
                    }
                }

                // Return as-is for external URLs or already-relative paths
                return url;
            },

            renderNews() {
                const container = document.getElementById('news-container');
                if (!container) {
                    return;
                }

                // Guard: only clear and re-render if we have actual data
                // This protects the static pre-rendered HTML cards from being wiped
                if (!this.newsData || this.newsData.length === 0) {
                    console.warn('[Admin] renderNews() skipped — no data yet. Static cards preserved.');
                    return;
                }

                // Clear container before rendering new data to avoid stale content
                container.innerHTML = '';

                // --- FAIR SPLIT LOGIC: 5 posts per author ---
                const groups = {};
                this.newsData.forEach(item => {
                    const author = item.author || 'unknown';
                    if (!groups[author]) groups[author] = [];
                    groups[author].push(item);
                });

                let balancedNews = [];
                Object.keys(groups).forEach(author => {
                    // Sort each author's group by timestamp
                    const authorPosts = groups[author].sort((a, b) => (parseInt(b.timestamp || 0) - parseInt(a.timestamp || 0)));
                    // Take top 5
                    balancedNews = balancedNews.concat(authorPosts.slice(0, 5));
                });

                // Final sort by timestamp descending for the combined 15 items
                const sortedNews = balancedNews.sort((a, b) => {
                    const ta = parseInt(a.timestamp || 0);
                    const tb = parseInt(b.timestamp || 0);
                    return tb - ta;
                }).slice(0, 15);

                container.innerHTML = sortedNews.map((item, index) => {
                    const shortcode = this.extractInstagramShortcode(item.link || '');
                    const articleId = shortcode ? shortcode : `art_${index}`;

                    return `
                    <div class="news-card ai-observable-card" data-article-id="${articleId}" data-news-index="${index}">
                        ${this.isLoggedIn ? `
                            <div style="position: absolute; top: 10px; right: 10px; z-index: 10; display: flex; gap: 5px;">
                                <button class="edit-btn" style="position: static; display: block;" onclick="Admin.openEdit(${index})"><i class="fas fa-edit"></i> Edit</button>
                                <button class="edit-btn" style="position: static; display: block; background: #e74c3c;" onclick="Admin.deleteNews(${index})"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i> Hapus</button>
                            </div>
                        ` : ''}
                        <div class="news-img-box">
                            ${this.renderMedia(item)}
                        </div>
                        <div class="news-details">
                            <div class="news-meta">
                                <span><i class="far fa-calendar-alt"></i> ${item.date}</span>
                                <span><i class="far fa-user"></i> ${item.author}</span>
                            </div>
                            <a href="${item.link}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" class="news-title" id="title_${articleId}">${item.title}</a>
                            <p class="news-excerpt" id="excerpt_${articleId}">${this.truncateText(item.excerpt, 150)}</p>
                            
                            <button class="read-more-btn" onclick="Admin.toggleArticle('${articleId}', ${index})" id="readmore_${articleId}">
                                Baca Selengkapnya <i class="fas fa-chevron-down"></i>
                            </button>

                            <div class="news-full-article" id="full-article_${articleId}" style="display:none; margin-top: 20px; text-align: left;">
                                ${item.excerpt}
                                
                                <div class="ai-article-container" id="article_container_${articleId}" style="margin-top: 20px; border-top: 1px solid #eee; padding-top:15px; font-size: 0.95rem; color: #444; line-height: 1.6;">
                                    <!-- AI Content will be injected here -->
                                </div>
                                
                                <!-- Share Buttons -->
                                <div class="share-buttons">
                                    <button class="share-btn wa" onclick="Admin.shareToWA('${item.title}', '${item.link}')">
                                        <i class="fab fa-whatsapp"></i> WhatsApp
                                    </button>
                                    <button class="share-btn fb" onclick="Admin.shareToFB('${item.link}')">
                                        <i class="fab fa-facebook"></i> Facebook
                                    </button>
                                    <button class="share-btn copy" onclick="Admin.copyLink('${item.link}')">
                                        <i class="fas fa-link"></i> Salin Link
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                `;
                }).join('');

                // Add Load More button container
                container.innerHTML += `
                    <div class="load-more-container" id="load-more-container">
                        <button class="load-more-btn" id="load-more-btn" onclick="Admin.loadMoreNews()">
                            <i class="fas fa-plus"></i> Muat Lebih Banyak ({{count}} artikel lagi)
                        </button>
                    </div>
                `;

                // Initialize load more state
                this.displayedCount = 10;
                this.totalCount = sortedNews.length;
                this.updateLoadMoreButton();

                // Hide articles beyond initial count
                this.applyLoadMore();

                // Call enforceAutoplay after content is updated
                this.enforceAutoplay();

                // Initialize AI Intersection Observer for generating articles
                this.initAIObserver();
            },

            async syncWithIndexData() {
                // Background sync - safe: keep showing existing articles during sync
                console.log("Syncing news data from server...");

                // Keep a backup of current news in case fetch fails
                const backupData = this.newsData ? [...this.newsData] : [];

                // Clear AI article cache to regenerate articles for new data
                if (this._articleCache) {
                    this._articleCache.clear();
                }

                // Fetch fresh data without clearing the view first
                try {
                    const cb = `?cb=${Date.now()}`;
                    const response = await fetch(this.apiPath + cb);
                    if (response.ok) {
                        const data = await response.json();
                        if (Array.isArray(data) && data.length > 0) {
                            this.newsData = data;
                            localStorage.setItem('rr_news_data', JSON.stringify(data));
                            this.renderNews();
                            console.log("News sync completed. Items:", data.length);
                        } else {
                            console.warn("Sync returned empty data, keeping current articles.");
                        }
                    } else {
                        console.warn("Sync fetch failed:", response.status, "- keeping current articles.");
                    }
                } catch (e) {
                    console.warn("Sync error, keeping current articles:", e.message);
                    // Restore backup if something went wrong
                    if (backupData.length > 0 && (!this.newsData || this.newsData.length === 0)) {
                        this.newsData = backupData;
                        this.renderNews();
                    }
                }
            },

            initAIObserver() {
                if (this._aiObserver) {
                    this._aiObserver.disconnect();
                }

                const options = {
                    root: null,
                    rootMargin: '0px',
                    threshold: 0.15 // Generate when 15% visible
                };

                this._aiObserver = new IntersectionObserver((entries, observer) => {
                    entries.forEach(entry => {
                        if (entry.isIntersecting) {
                            const card = entry.target;
                            const articleId = card.getAttribute('data-article-id');
                            const index = card.getAttribute('data-news-index');

                            // Prevent re-generation or multiple calls
                            if (!this._articleCache.has(articleId) && !card.classList.contains('ai-loading')) {
                                card.classList.add('ai-loading');
                                this.generateAIArticle(articleId, this.newsData[index]);
                            }
                            // Stop observing once triggered
                            observer.unobserve(card);
                        }
                    });
                }, options);

                document.querySelectorAll('.ai-observable-card').forEach(card => {
                    const articleId = card.getAttribute('data-article-id');
                    if (!this._articleCache.has(articleId)) {
                        this._aiObserver.observe(card);
                    } else {
                        // Restore from cache if already generated
                        const container = document.getElementById(`article_container_${articleId}`);
                        if (container) container.innerHTML = this._articleCache.get(articleId);
                    }
                });
            },

            async generateAIArticle(articleId, newsItem) {
                const container = document.getElementById(`article_container_${articleId}`);
                if (!container) return;

                // 1. Show IMPROVED Skeleton Shimmer
                container.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="ai-loading-skeleton">
                        <div class="skeleton-line title"></div>
                        <div class="skeleton-line"></div>
                        <div class="skeleton-line"></div>
                        <div class="skeleton-line short"></div>
                        <div class="skeleton-line"></div>
                        <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; margin-top: 15px; color: #888; font-size: 0.85rem;">
                            <i class="fas fa-robot fa-spin" style="color: var(--accent);"></i>
                            <span>AI sedang menulis artikel...</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                `;

                // 2. Prepare Prompt
                const prompt = `Kamu adalah jurnalis sekolah resmi BP3NU Raden Rahmat Balongbendo, Sidoarjo.
Tulis artikel berita berdasarkan data postingan Instagram berikut.
DATA: Akun: ${newsItem.author}, Judul: ${newsItem.title}, Ringkasan: ${newsItem.excerpt}, Tanggal: ${newsItem.date}, Link: ${newsItem.link}
KETENTUAN: Panjang 300-500 kata. Bahasa Indonesia baku. Struktur: lead → isi (2-3 paragraf) → penutup.
Gunakan <h2> untuk judul bagian, <p> untuk paragraf, <strong> untuk kata kunci.
Sertakan konteks BP3NU Raden Rahmat sebagai lembaga pendidikan Islami berakreditasi A di Balongbendo, Sidoarjo.
Output HANYA HTML murni (p, h2, strong) tanpa backtick atau markdown.`;

                try {
                    // Call backend proxy API
                    const response = await fetch('api/anthropic_proxy.php', {
                        method: 'POST',
                        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                        body: JSON.stringify({ prompt: prompt })
                    });

                    if (!response.ok) throw new Error('API request failed');
                    const data = await response.json();

                    let aiHtml = '';
                    if (data.content && data.content[0] && data.content[0].text) {
                        aiHtml = data.content[0].text;
                    } else {
                        aiHtml = "<p><em>Gagal menulis laporan, server sibuk.</em></p>";
                        throw new Error("Invalid response format");
                    }

                    // Remove markdown wrapper if accidentally included
                    aiHtml = aiHtml.replace(/\`\`\`html/g, '').replace(/\`\`\`/g, '');

                    // Enhance Arabic text styling
                    aiHtml = aiHtml.replace(/<p>([^\u0600-\u06FF]*[\u0600-\u06FF][^\u0600-\u06FF]*)<\/p>/g,
                        '<p class="arabic-text">$1</p>');

                    // Word count estimation
                    const wordCount = aiHtml.replace(/<[^>]*>?/gm, '').split(/\s+/).length;

                    // 3. Render Final Box
                    const renderedResult = `
                        <div class="ai-content">
                            ${aiHtml}
                        </div>
                        <div class="ai-footer-box" style="margin-top: 20px; padding: 15px; background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(0, 104, 55, 0.05), rgba(243, 156, 18, 0.05)); border-radius: 12px; border-left: 4px solid var(--primary); font-size: 0.85rem; line-height: 1.5;">
                            <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 15px;">
                                <div>
                                    <strong style="color: var(--primary); font-size: 0.9rem;"><i class="fab fa-instagram"></i> <a href="${newsItem.link}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">@${newsItem.author}</a></strong><br>
                                    <span style="color: #666;"><i class="far fa-calendar-alt"></i> ${newsItem.date}</span>
                                </div>
                                <div style="text-align: right;">
                                    <span style="background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(243, 156, 18, 0.2), rgba(243, 156, 18, 0.1)); color: var(--accent-dark); padding: 6px 12px; border-radius: 15px; font-weight: 600; font-size: 0.8rem;"><i class="fas fa-file-alt"></i> ${wordCount} kata</span>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            <div style="margin-top: 12px; font-style: italic; color: #888; text-align: right; border-top: 1px dashed #ddd; padding-top: 10px; font-size: 0.75rem;">
                                <i class="fas fa-robot"></i> Ditulis otomatis oleh Gemini AI 2.5 (Google Flash)
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    `;

                    container.innerHTML = renderedResult;
                    this._articleCache.set(articleId, renderedResult);

                } catch (e) {
                    console.error("AI Generation Error: ", e);
                    // Fallback using original excerpt
                    const fallbackHtml = `
                        <p style="color: #666; font-style: italic;">${newsItem.excerpt}</p>
                        <a href="${newsItem.link}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="color: var(--accent); font-weight: 700; display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 5px;">
                            Lihat Sumber <i class="fas fa-chevron-right"></i>
                        </a>
                    `;
                    container.innerHTML = fallbackHtml;
                    this._articleCache.set(articleId, fallbackHtml); // Cache fallback too so it doesn't loop
                }
            },

            openLogin() {
                if (this.isLoggedIn) return;
                document.getElementById('admin-modal').style.display = 'flex';
                document.getElementById('modal-title').innerText = 'Admin Login (Secure)';
                document.getElementById('login-form').style.display = 'block';
                document.getElementById('edit-form').style.display = 'none';
                document.getElementById('profile-form').style.display = 'none';
            },

            async login() {
                const user = document.getElementById('admin-user').value;
                const pass = document.getElementById('admin-pass').value;

                if (!user || !pass) {
                    alert('Username dan Password wajib diisi!');
                    return;
                }

                try {
                    const res = await fetch(`${this.authPath}?action=login`, {
                        method: 'POST',
                        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                        body: JSON.stringify({ user, pass })
                    });
                    const data = await res.json();

                    if (data.success) {
                        this.isLoggedIn = true;
                        this.currentUser = user;
                        // Fetch full user info to get role
                        const checkRes = await fetch(`${this.authPath}?action=check`);
                        const checkData = await checkRes.json();
                        this.userRole = checkData.role || 'admin';

                        this.closeModal();
                        document.getElementById('admin-info').innerText = `MODE ${this.userRole.toUpperCase()} AKTIF (${user})`;
                        document.getElementById('admin-banner').style.display = 'flex';

                        // Show toggle button
                        const toggleBtn = document.querySelector('.admin-toggle-btn');
                        if (toggleBtn) toggleBtn.style.display = 'flex';

                        this.renderNews();
                        alert(`Login Berhasil! Selamat datang, ${user}.`);
                    } else {
                        alert(data.message || 'Username atau Password salah!');
                    }
                } catch (e) {
                    alert("Gagal terhubung ke server login.");
                }
            },

            async logout() {
                if (confirm('Apakah anda yakin ingin keluar dari Mode Admin?')) {
                    try {
                        await fetch(`${this.authPath}?action=logout`);
                        this.isLoggedIn = false;
                        document.getElementById('admin-banner').style.display = 'none';

                        // Hide toggle button
                        const toggleBtn = document.querySelector('.admin-toggle-btn');
                        if (toggleBtn) toggleBtn.style.display = 'none';

                        this.renderNews();
                        document.getElementById('admin-user').value = '';
                        document.getElementById('admin-pass').value = '';
                    } catch (e) {
                        alert("Gagal logout dari server.");
                    }
                }
            },

            openEdit(index) {
                const item = this.newsData[index];
                document.getElementById('admin-modal').style.display = 'flex';
                document.getElementById('modal-title').innerText = 'Edit Berita';
                document.getElementById('login-form').style.display = 'none';
                document.getElementById('edit-form').style.display = 'block';
                document.getElementById('profile-form').style.display = 'none';

                document.getElementById('edit-index').value = index;
                document.getElementById('edit-title').value = item.title;

                // Set logic for img input
                // If existing image is local (uploads/...), maybe prefer keeping it as is unless new upload
                // We'll set the URL field regardless, but default view to 'url' mode if it looks like a URL
                // Actually, let's just reset the upload input
                document.getElementById('file-upload').value = '';
                document.getElementById('edit-img').value = item.img;
                this.updatePreview(item.img);

                // Default to URL mode if there's an existing image so they can see the link
                // But if they want to upload, they switch tab.
                // Let's default to 'url' so they see the current value.
                this.switchUpload('url');

                document.getElementById('edit-excerpt').value = item.excerpt;
                document.getElementById('edit-link').value = item.link;
            },

            openAdd() {
                document.getElementById('admin-modal').style.display = 'flex';
                document.getElementById('modal-title').innerText = 'Tambah Berita';
                document.getElementById('login-form').style.display = 'none';
                document.getElementById('edit-form').style.display = 'block';
                document.getElementById('profile-form').style.display = 'none';

                document.getElementById('edit-index').value = 'new';
                document.getElementById('edit-title').value = '';

                document.getElementById('file-upload').value = '';
                document.getElementById('edit-img').value = '';
                this.updatePreview('');
                this.switchUpload('local'); // Default to upload for new items

                document.getElementById('edit-excerpt').value = '';
                document.getElementById('edit-link').value = '';
            },

            openProfile() {
                document.getElementById('admin-modal').style.display = 'flex';
                document.getElementById('modal-title').innerText = 'Pengaturan Profil';
                document.getElementById('login-form').style.display = 'none';
                document.getElementById('edit-form').style.display = 'none';
                document.getElementById('profile-form').style.display = 'block';

                document.getElementById('new-user').value = this.currentUser;
                document.getElementById('new-pass').value = '';
            },

            async updateProfile() {
                const newUser = document.getElementById('new-user').value;
                const newPass = document.getElementById('new-pass').value;

                if (!newUser || !newPass) return alert('Data tidak boleh kosong!');

                try {
                    const res = await fetch(`${this.authPath}?action = update_pass`, {
                        method: 'POST',
                        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                        body: JSON.stringify({ username: newUser, new_pass: newPass })
                    });
                    const result = await res.json();
                    if (result.success) {
                        this.currentUser = newUser;
                        document.getElementById('admin-info').innerText = `MODE ADMIN AKTIF (${newUser})`;
                        this.closeModal();
                        alert('Profil berhasil diperbarui!');
                    } else {
                        alert("Gagal memperbarui profil: " + result.message);
                    }
                } catch (e) {
                    alert("Gagal terhubung ke server.");
                }
            },

            // Helper: Upload File
            async uploadFile(file) {
                const formData = new FormData();
                formData.append('file', file);

                try {
                    const res = await fetch(this.uploadPath, {
                        method: 'POST',
                        body: formData
                    });
                    const data = await res.json();
                    if (data.success) {
                        return data.url;
                    } else {
                        throw new Error(data.error);
                    }
                } catch (e) {
                    alert("Upload Gagal: " + e.message);
                    return null;
                }
            },

            async save() {
                const index = document.getElementById('edit-index').value;
                let finalImgUrl = document.getElementById('edit-img').value; // Default to URL input

                // If Local mode and file selected, upload it
                if (this.uploadMode === 'local') {
                    const fileInput = document.getElementById('file-upload');
                    if (fileInput.files.length > 0) {
                        const uploadedUrl = await this.uploadFile(fileInput.files[0]);
                        if (!uploadedUrl) return; // Stop if upload failed
                        finalImgUrl = uploadedUrl;
                    }
                    // If no file selected in local mode, we might want to keep the old image?
                    // If index != new, and no file, we keep the old one.
                    // But wait, where is the old one? It's in newsData[index].img
                    // But if we are in local mode and input is empty, and we hit save, what happens?
                    // We should probably rely on the hidden URL field being populated?
                    // No, simpler: check if file provided. If not, use what's in 'edit-img' (which might be old value if we populated it)
                    // BUT, I didn't populate 'edit-img' with existing value in openAdd().
                    // In openEdit(), I did: document.getElementById('edit-img').value = item.img;
                    // So if they strictly switch to local tab and don't pick a file, we want to retain the old image.
                    // The old image URL is currently sitting in #edit-img.
                    // So if fileInput is empty, finalImgUrl remains #edit-img value. Correct.
                }

                const newData = {
                    title: document.getElementById('edit-title').value,
                    img: finalImgUrl || 'https://picsum.photos/seed/news/400/250',
                    excerpt: document.getElementById('edit-excerpt').value,
                    link: document.getElementById('edit-link').value || '#',
                    date: new Date().toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' }),
                    author: this.currentUser
                };

                if (index === 'new') {
                    this.newsData.unshift(newData);
                } else {
                    const idx = parseInt(index);
                    this.newsData[idx] = { ...this.newsData[idx], ...newData };
                }

                // OPTIMISTIC UI: Render immediately
                this.renderNews();
                this.closeModal();

                // SAVE TO SERVER
                try {
                    const res = await fetch(this.apiPath, {
                        method: 'POST',
                        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                        body: JSON.stringify({ all_news: this.newsData })
                    });
                    const result = await res.json();

                    if (result.success) {
                        localStorage.setItem('rr_news_data', JSON.stringify(this.newsData));
                        console.log("Server save successful:", result);
                    } else {
                        throw new Error(result.error || 'Unknown server error');
                    }
                } catch (e) {
                    console.error("Save failed:", e);
                    alert("Gagal sinkronisasi ke server: " + e.message + " (Data tersimpan secara lokal sementara)");
                }
            },

            deleteNews(index) {
                if (confirm(`Apakah Anda yakin ingin menghapus berita: "${this.newsData[index].title}" ? `)) {
                    this.newsData.splice(index, 1);

                    // SAVE TO SERVER
                    fetch(this.apiPath, {
                        method: 'POST',
                        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                        body: JSON.stringify({ all_news: this.newsData })
                    })
                        .then(res => res.json())
                        .then(data => {
                            if (data.success) {
                                localStorage.setItem('rr_news_data', JSON.stringify(this.newsData));
                                this.renderNews();
                            } else {
                                alert("Gagal menghapus.");
                            }
                        })
                        .catch(e => alert("Error koneksi: " + e.message));
                }
            },

            closeModal() {
                document.getElementById('admin-modal').style.display = 'none';
            },

            // === ADMIN UI TOGGLE (NEW) ===
            toggleAdminBanner() {
                const banner = document.getElementById('admin-banner');
                const btn = document.querySelector('.admin-toggle-btn');

                if (!banner) return;

                const isHidden = banner.classList.contains('hidden');

                if (isHidden) {
                    banner.classList.remove('hidden');
                    if (btn) btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-eye-slash"></i>';
                } else {
                    banner.classList.add('hidden');
                    if (btn) btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-eye"></i>';
                }
            }
        };

        // Expose Admin ke scope global agar onclick handlers di HTML dapat mengaksesnya
        window.Admin = Admin;

        // Jalankan setelah DOM selesai dimuat
        if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
                try {
                    Admin.init();
                } catch (e) {
                    console.error("CRITICAL: Admin module initialization failed!", e);
                }
            });
        } else {
            // DOM sudah siap (script berjalan setelah </body>)
            try {
                Admin.init();
            } catch (e) {
                console.error("CRITICAL: Admin module initialization failed!", e);
            }
        }

        // ── GA4 Traffic Widget Fetcher ─────────────────────────────────────────
        // Mengambil data dari api/live_stats.php (Google Analytics 4)
        // Fallback ke api/visitor_count.php jika GA4 tidak tersedia.
        async function updateVisitorStats() {
            try {
                // ── Primary: GA4 live_stats.php ──────────────────────────────
                const res = await fetch('api/live_stats.php?_=' + Date.now());
                if (!res.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + res.status);
                const data = await res.json();

                const elOnline = document.getElementById('widget-online');
                const elToday = document.getElementById('widget-today');
                const elTotal = document.getElementById('widget-total');
                const elChart = document.getElementById('widgetChart');

                // Update Footer Stats (elemen lama, tetap didukung)
                if (document.getElementById('total-v')) document.getElementById('total-v').innerText = data.total_fmt || data.total_sessions || '0';
                if (document.getElementById('today-v')) document.getElementById('today-v').innerText = data.today_fmt || data.today_sessions || '0';
                if (document.getElementById('online-v')) document.getElementById('online-v').innerText = data.active_users_fmt || '0';

                // Update Widget KPI
                if (elOnline) elOnline.textContent = data.active_users_fmt ?? '0';
                if (elToday) elToday.textContent = data.today_fmt ?? '0';
                if (elTotal) elTotal.textContent = data.total_fmt ?? '0';

                // Update Widget Chart (GA4 7-day bar chart)
                if (elChart && Array.isArray(data.chart_7d) && data.chart_7d.length > 0) {
                    elChart.innerHTML = data.chart_7d.map((bar, i) => {
                        const pct = bar.height_pct || 5;
                        const label = bar.label || (i + 1);
                        const tip = `${label}: ${bar.sessions ?? 0} sesi`;
                        const isToday = (i === data.chart_7d.length - 1);
                        // Warna: hijau = tinggi, biru = sedang, abu = rendah, kuning = hari ini
                        const color = isToday
                            ? 'linear-gradient(to top, #f59e0b, #fcd34d)'
                            : pct >= 70
                                ? 'linear-gradient(to top, #16a34a, #4ade80)'
                                : pct >= 30
                                    ? 'linear-gradient(to top, #2563eb, #60a5fa)'
                                    : 'linear-gradient(to top, #475569, #94a3b8)';
                        return `<div class="bar-wrap" style="flex:1;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;height:100%;justify-content:flex-end;">
                            <div class="traffic-bar${isToday ? ' active' : ''}"
                                 title="${tip}"
                                 style="height:${pct}%;width:100%;background:${color};border-radius:3px 3px 0 0;min-height:3px;transition:height 0.5s ease;">
                            </div>
                            <div style="font-size:7px;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.35);margin-top:2px;">${label}</div>
                        </div>`;
                    }).join('');
                }

                // Animasi kilat saat nilai diperbarui
                [elOnline, elToday, elTotal].forEach(el => {
                    if (!el) return;
                    el.style.transition = 'color 0.3s ease';
                    el.style.color = '#4ade80';
                    setTimeout(() => { el.style.color = ''; }, 600);
                });

            } catch (e) {
                // ── Fallback: visitor_count.php ───────────────────────────────
                console.warn('[TrafficWidget] GA4 API tidak tersedia, fallback ke visitor_count.php:', e.message);
                try {
                    const res2 = await fetch('api/visitor_count.php');
                    const data2 = await res2.json();

                    if (document.getElementById('total-v')) document.getElementById('total-v').innerText = (data2.total || 0).toLocaleString();
                    if (document.getElementById('today-v')) document.getElementById('today-v').innerText = (data2.today || 0).toLocaleString();
                    if (document.getElementById('online-v')) document.getElementById('online-v').innerText = data2.online || '1';

                    if (document.getElementById('widget-total')) document.getElementById('widget-total').innerText = (data2.total || 0).toLocaleString();
                    if (document.getElementById('widget-today')) document.getElementById('widget-today').innerText = (data2.today || 0).toLocaleString();
                    if (document.getElementById('widget-online')) document.getElementById('widget-online').innerText = data2.online || '1';

                    const chartContainer = document.getElementById('widgetChart');
                    if (data2.history && chartContainer) {
                        chartContainer.innerHTML = '';
                        const max = Math.max(...data2.history, 5);
                        data2.history.forEach((val, i) => {
                            const h = Math.max((val / max) * 100, 5);
                            const bar = document.createElement('div');
                            bar.className = 'traffic-bar' + (i === data2.history.length - 1 ? ' active' : '');
                            bar.style.height = h + '%';
                            bar.title = `Hari ${i + 1}: ${val}`;
                            chartContainer.appendChild(bar);
                        });
                    }
                } catch (e2) {
                    console.error('[TrafficWidget] Semua sumber statistik gagal:', e2.message);
                }
            }
        }

        // Load pertama
        updateVisitorStats();

        // Auto-refresh setiap 2 menit (sinkron dengan cache TTL server)
        setInterval(updateVisitorStats, 120_000);
    </script>

    <script src="js/dynamic_loader.js"></script>
    <script src="js/quick_access.js?v=2"></script>

        fetch('/chat-widget.html')
            .then(res => res.text())
            .then(html => {
                const container = document.getElementById('rr-chat-widget-container');
                container.innerHTML = html;
                const scripts = container.querySelectorAll('script');
                scripts.forEach(oldScript => {
                    const newScript = document.createElement('script');
                    Array.from(oldScript.attributes).forEach(attr => newScript.setAttribute(attr.name, attr.value));
                    newScript.appendChild(document.createTextNode(oldScript.innerHTML));
                    oldScript.parentNode.replaceChild(newScript, oldScript);
                });
            });
    </script>

    <script>
        const menuBtn = document.getElementById('menu-btn');
        const navList = document.getElementById('nav-list');
        const navBar = document.querySelector('.nav-bar');
        let lastScroll = 0;

        menuBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            navList.classList.toggle('active');
            const icon = menuBtn.querySelector('i');
            if (navList.classList.contains('active')) {
                icon.classList.remove('fa-bars');
                icon.classList.add('fa-times');
            } else {
                icon.classList.remove('fa-times');
                icon.classList.add('fa-bars');
            }
        });

        // Auto-hide Nav-bar on mobile scroll
        window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
            if (window.innerWidth <= 768) {
                const currentScroll = window.pageYOffset;

                // Don't hide if menu is open
                if (navList.classList.contains('active')) return;

                if (currentScroll <= 0) {
                    navBar.classList.remove('nav-hidden');
                    return;
                }

                if (currentScroll > lastScroll && !navBar.classList.contains('nav-hidden') && currentScroll > 100) {
                    // Scrolling Down
                    navBar.classList.add('nav-hidden');
                } else if (currentScroll < lastScroll && navBar.classList.contains('nav-hidden')) {
                    // Scrolling Up
                    navBar.classList.remove('nav-hidden');
                }
                lastScroll = currentScroll;
            }
        });

        // Dropdown Toggle for Mobile
        const dropdownToggles = document.querySelectorAll('.has-dropdown > a');
        dropdownToggles.forEach(toggle => {
            toggle.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                if (window.innerWidth <= 768) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    const dropdown = toggle.nextElementSibling;
                    dropdown.classList.toggle('show');
                }
            });
        });

        // Current Date Update
        const options = { weekday: 'long', year: 'numeric', month: 'long', day: 'numeric' };
        document.getElementById('current-date').textContent = new Date().toLocaleDateString('id-ID', options);
    </script>
